    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {

    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

  } catch (e) {}

mb-14" data-page-title="Use the W&amp;B MCP Server" data-page-href="/platform/mcp-server" id="content"><span data-as="p">Model Context Protocol (MCP) is an open standard that lets AI agents call external tools. The W&amp;B MCP Server gives your IDE, coding assistant, or chat agent direct access to your W&amp;B data and documentation. With this access, your agent can answer questions about your runs, traces, evaluations, and artifacts without copy-paste. For a full list of what you can do with the server, see the <a href="#w&amp;b-mcp-server-capabilities" class="link">W&amp;B MCP Server capabilities</a> section.</span>
<span data-as="p">It integrates directly with most IDEs, coding clients, and chat agents, including:</span>
<ul>
<li>Cursor</li>
<li>Visual Studio Code (VS Code)</li>
<li>Claude Code</li>
<li>Codex</li>
<li>Gemini CLI</li>
<li>Mistral LeChat</li>
<li>Claude Desktop</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="deployment-types"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#deployment-types" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Deployment types</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The W&amp;B MCP Server is available in two deployment options. Use the hosted server for the fastest setup, or set up a local version if you need more isolation and flexibility. The local version requires your client to use a different URL to access the server.</span>
<div class="columns prose dark:prose-invert grid max-w-none gap-4 @2xl/columns-container:grid-cols-[repeat(var(--cols),minmax(0,1fr))] @[0px]/columns-container:grid-cols-1 card-group dark:prose-dark gap-y-0 sm:grid-cols-[repeat(var(--cols),minmax(0,1fr))]!" style="--cols:2"><div class="card block font-normal group relative my-2 ring-2 ring-transparent rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-background-dark border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 overflow-hidden w-full"><div class="px-6 py-5 relative" data-component-part="card-content-container"><div class="w-full"><h2 id="_R_11cljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_" class="not-prose font-semibold text-base text-gray-800 dark:text-white" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="card-title">Hosted server (recommended)</h2><div id="_R_11cljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutbH1_" class="prose mt-1 font-normal text-base leading-6 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400" data-component-part="card-content"><span data-as="p">A W&amp;B-managed MCP server that your client connects to over HTTP with your W&amp;B API key. No installation, no local process to maintain.</span><span data-as="p"><a href="#use-the-hosted-server" class="link">Use the hosted server</a></span></div></div></div></div><div class="card block font-normal group relative my-2 ring-2 ring-transparent rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-background-dark border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 overflow-hidden w-full"><div class="px-6 py-5 relative" data-component-part="card-content-container"><div class="w-full"><h2 id="_R_21cljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_" class="not-prose font-semibold text-base text-gray-800 dark:text-white" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="card-title">Local install</h2><div id="_R_21cljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutbH1_" class="prose mt-1 font-normal text-base leading-6 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400" data-component-part="card-content"><span data-as="p">Run the MCP server on your own machine over STDIO or HTTP. Use when you need air-gapped operation, pinning to a specific release, custom server behavior, active server development, or support for a client that only speaks STDIO.</span><span data-as="p"><a href="#run-the-mcp-server-locally" class="link">Run the MCP server locally</a></span></div></div></div></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="prerequisites"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#prerequisites" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Prerequisites</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Before you configure any client, make sure you have the following in place:</span>
<ul>
<li>Create a W&amp;B API key at <a href="https://wandb.ai/authorize" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">wandb.ai/authorize</a>.</li>
<li>Set the key as the <code>WANDB_API_KEY</code> environment variable, or pass it to your client as a bearer token.</li>
<li>For Dedicated Cloud, Self-Managed, and local installs against a non-default instance, set the <code>WANDB_BASE_URL</code> environment variable to your instance URL.</li>
<li>W&amp;B pins the <code>mcp</code> SDK to <a href="https://pypi.org/project/mcp/1.14.0/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">1.14.0</a>, release <code>2024-11-05</code>. Clients must connect using <code>mcp</code> SDK 1.14.x. For streamable HTTP support in W&amp;B Dedicated Cloud, <code>mcp</code> SDK 1.14.x release <code>2025-03-26</code> or newer is required.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="use-the-hosted-server"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#use-the-hosted-server" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Use the hosted server</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">W&amp;B runs a managed MCP server for every deployment type. You don’t need to install anything. Configure your client to connect over HTTP with a W&amp;B API key in the <code>Authorization</code> header.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="connection-url"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#connection-url" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Connection URL</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The URL depends on your type of W&amp;B deployment:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_3cljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Deployment</th><th>Server URL</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Multi-tenant Cloud</td><td><code>https://mcp.withwandb.com/mcp</code></td></tr><tr><td>Dedicated Cloud</td><td><code>https://[YOUR-INSTANCE]/mcp</code></td></tr><tr><td>Self-Managed</td><td><code>https://[YOUR-INSTANCE]/mcp</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">For Dedicated Cloud or Self-Managed, replace <code>https://mcp.withwandb.com/mcp</code> with <code>https://[YOUR-INSTANCE]/mcp</code> and keep everything else the same. The following client configurations use the Multi-tenant URL.</span>

style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> wandb</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> https://mcp.withwandb.com/mcp</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D7BA7D"> </code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><span data-as="p">Add <code>--scope user</code> to configure Claude Code globally. Omit it to configure only the current project.</span><span data-as="p">Verify the connection by asking <code>List my W&amp;B entities.</code> The agent should call <code>list_entities_tool</code> and return your username and any teams. If the connection fails, see <a href="#troubleshooting" class="link">Troubleshooting</a>. For more information, see <a href="https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code/mcp" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Claude Code’s MCP documentation</a>.</span></div><div id="panel-claude-desktop-1" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="claude-desktop" tabindex="0" class="prose dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto [&amp;_[data-table-wrapper]]:![--page-padding:0px] [&amp;_[role=&quot;listitem&quot;]]:pl-4 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)_img]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child[data-table-wrapper]]:pt-0! hidden" data-component-part="tab-content"><span data-as="p">Claude Desktop’s built-in custom connectors interface doesn’t support API key authentication for remote MCP servers. To work around this, use the <a href="https://www.npmjs.com/package/mcp-remote" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>mcp-remote</code></a> npm proxy to connect Claude Desktop to the W&amp;B remote MCP server. The proxy runs locally and forwards requests to <code>https://mcp.withwandb.com/mcp</code> with your bearer token.</span><span data-as="p">You need <a href="https://nodejs.org/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Node.js</a> installed on your system.</span><span data-as="p">Open your Claude Desktop config file in a text editor. You can find the config file at the following location for your OS:</span><ul>
<li><strong>macOS</strong>: <code>~/Library/Application\ Support/Claude/claude_desktop_config.json</code></li>
<li><strong>Windows</strong>: <code>%APPDATA%\Claude\claude_desktop_config.json</code></li>
</ul><span data-as="p">Add the following to the JSON object in your config file, replacing <code>[YOUR-WANDB-API-KEY]</code> with your W&amp;B API key:</span><div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent <li>
<span data-as="p">On macOS, open <strong>Cursor</strong> &gt; <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>Cursor Settings</strong>. On Windows or Linux, open <strong>Preferences</strong> &gt; <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>Cursor Settings</strong>.</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p">Select <strong>Tools and MCP</strong>.</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p">In <strong>Installed MCP Servers</strong>, select <strong>Add Custom MCP</strong>. Cursor opens the <code>mcp.json</code> configuration file.</span>
</li>
<li>

For more information, see <a href="https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/mcp-server/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Gemini CLI’s MCP documentation</a>.</span></div><div id="panel-mistral-lechat-5" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="mistral-lechat" tabindex="0" class="prose dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto [&amp;_[data-table-wrapper]]:![--page-padding:0px] [&amp;_[role=&quot;listitem&quot;]]:pl-4 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)_img]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child[data-table-wrapper]]:pt-0! hidden" data-component-part="tab-content"><ol>
<li>In LeChat, open the <strong>Intelligence</strong> menu and select <strong>Add Connector</strong>.</li>
<li>Select <strong>Custom MCP Connector</strong>.</li>
<li>Configure the following fields:<!-- -->
<ul>
<li><strong>Connector Server</strong>: <code>https://mcp.withwandb.com/mcp</code></li>
<li><strong>Description</strong>: (Optional) A short description.</li>
<li><strong>Authentication Method</strong>: Select <strong>API Token Authentication</strong>.</li>
<li><strong>Header name</strong>: Leave as <code>Authorization</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Header type</strong>: Select <strong>Bearer</strong>.</li>
<li><strong>Header value</strong>: Your W&amp;B API key.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Select <strong>Create</strong>.</li>
<li>Verify the connection by asking <code>List my W&amp;B entities.</code> The agent should call <code>list_entities_tool</code> and return your username and any teams.</li>
</ol><span data-as="p">If the connection fails, see <a href="#troubleshooting" class="link">Troubleshooting</a>. For more information, see <a href="https://mistral.ai/news/le-chat-mcp-connectors-memories" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">LeChat’s MCP documentation</a>.</span></div><div <h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="run-the-mcp-server-locally"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#run-the-mcp-server-locally" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Run the MCP server locally</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">A local install is an alternative to the hosted server, not the default for any deployment type. Use it when the hosted server doesn’t fit your setup.</span>
<span data-as="p">Common reasons to run locally:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Air-gapped or offline environments</strong> where your client can’t reach a hosted W&amp;B endpoint.</li>
<li><strong>Pinned version</strong>. The hosted server follows the main branch. A local install can pin to a specific release tag.</li>
<li><strong>Custom server behavior</strong> such as changing tool descriptions, adding tools, or setting a non-default response token budget.</li>
<li><strong>Active development</strong> on the server itself.</li>
<li><strong>STDIO-only clients</strong> or clients that require a local process.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">For Dedicated Cloud or Self-Managed users, prefer the hosted path. Only use a local install from <a href="https://github.com/wandb/wandb-mcp-server" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">wandb/wandb-mcp-server</a> if the hosted server isn’t yet enabled on your instance or one of the preceding reasons applies. Set a <code>WANDB_BASE_URL</code> environment variable to your instance URL.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="local-prerequisites"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#local-prerequisites" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Local prerequisites</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To run the server locally, make sure you have the following:</span>
<ul>
<li>Python 3.11 or higher.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.astral.sh/uv/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>uv</code></a> or <code>pip</code>.</li>
<li>A W&amp;B API key, set as <code>WANDB_API_KEY</code>.</li>
<li><code>WANDB_BASE_URL</code> set to your instance URL if you use Dedicated Cloud or Self-Managed.</li>
</ul>

<span data-as="p">Update your MCP client configuration to use the tunnel URL.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="environment-variables"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#environment-variables" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Environment variables</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The following environment variables control authentication, instance routing, and server behavior for local installs. Set them in your client’s <code>env</code> block or export them in your shell.</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_9kljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Variable</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>WANDB_API_KEY</code></td><td>W&amp;B API key for authentication. Required.</td></tr><tr><td><code>WANDB_BASE_URL</code></td><td>Custom W&amp;B instance URL for Dedicated Cloud or Self-Managed. Defaults to <code>https://api.wandb.ai</code>.</td></tr><tr><td><code>WANDB_MCP_PROXY_DOCS</code></td><td>Enable the <code>search_wandb_docs_tool</code> documentation search proxy. Default: <code>true</code>.</td></tr><tr><td><code>WANDBOT_BASE_URL</code></td><td>Custom endpoint for the docs search proxy.</td></tr><tr><td><code>MAX_RESPONSE_TOKENS</code></td><td>Token budget for tool-response truncation. Default: <code>30000</code>.</td></tr><tr><td><code>MCP_SERVER_LOG_LEVEL</code></td><td>Logging verbosity. One of <code>DEBUG</code>, <code>INFO</code>, <code>WARNING</code>, <code>ERROR</code>.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">For the complete command-line reference and advanced options, see the <a href="https://github.com/wandb/wandb-mcp-server#readme" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">wandb-mcp-server README</a>.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="w&amp;b-mcp-server-capabilities"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#w&amp;b-mcp-server-capabilities" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">W&amp;B MCP Server capabilities</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Use the MCP server to analyze experiments, debug traces, create reports, manage registry and artifacts, and answer questions from the W&amp;B docs. The following example prompts demonstrate some of the tasks you can ask your agent to perform when it’s connected to the W&amp;B MCP Server:</span>
<ul>
<li>“Show me the top 5 runs by <code>eval/accuracy</code> in <code>your-team/your-project</code>.”</li>
<li>“How did the latency of my hiring agent’s predict traces evolve over the last month?”</li>
<li>“Generate a W&amp;B report comparing decisions made by the hiring agent last week.”</li>
<li>“What versions of the <code>production-model</code> artifact exist, and what changed between <code>v2</code> and <code>v3</code>?”</li>
<li>“How do I create a leaderboard in Weave?”</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="available-tools"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#available-tools" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Available tools</span></h3>

[&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Tool</th><th>Use when</th><th>Example prompt</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>query_wandb_tool</code></td><td>The question is about runs, sweeps, configs, summaries, or artifacts in W&amp;B Models. Runs a GraphQL query.</td><td>”Show me the top 5 runs by <code>eval/accuracy</code> in <code>your-team/your-project</code>.”</td></tr><tr><td><code>get_run_history_tool</code></td><td>The question is about training curves, metric trends over time, or any time-series logged to a run.</td><td>”Plot the loss curve for run <code>abc123</code> in <code>your-team/your-project</code>.”</td></tr><tr><td><code>compare_runs_tool</code></td><td>The question is what changed between two runs, or which of two runs is better. Returns config diff, metric delta, and optional aligned history.</td><td>”Compare runs <code>abc123</code> and <code>def456</code> in <code>your-team/your-project</code>.”</td></tr><tr><td><code>diagnose_run_tool</code></td><td>The question is whether a run converged, is overfitting, or hit NaN values. Returns specific recommendations.</td><td>”Is run <code>abc123</code> in <code>your-team/your-project</code> overfitting?”</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div></div><div id="panel-weave-traces-2" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="weave-traces" tabindex="0" class="prose dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto [&amp;_[data-table-wrapper]]:![--page-padding:0px] [&amp;_[role=&quot;listitem&quot;]]:pl-4 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)_img]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child[data-table-wrapper]]:pt-0! hidden" data-component-part="tab-content"><span data-as="p">Tools that query and aggregate LLM traces and evaluations.</span><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_2ebcljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Tool</th><th>Use when</th><th>Example prompt</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>query_weave_traces_tool</code></td><td>Trace data is required (LLM calls, evaluations, agent runs). Start with <code>detail_level=&quot;summary&quot;</code> and escalate to <code>&quot;full&quot;</code> only for specific traces.</td><td>”Show me failed traces in <code>your-team/your-project</code> from the last 24 hours.”</td></tr><tr><td><code>count_weave_traces_tool</code></td><td>The question is how many traces or how many errors, and the trace data itself isn’t needed.</td><td>”How many traces failed in <code>your-team/your-project</code> this week?”</td></tr><tr><td><code>resolve_trace_roots_tool</code></td><td>After <code>query_weave_traces_tool</code> finds child traces, to map each back to its root session or workflow in one batched call.</td><td>”Find LLM calls that contain <code>rate limit</code> and tell me which sessions they belong to.”</td></tr><tr><td><code>summarize_evaluation_tool</code></td><td>The question is how an eval went, what the pass rate is, or which tasks fail most. Aggregates <code>Evaluation.evaluate</code> hierarchies.</td><td>”Summarize the most recent eval in <code>your-team/your-project</code>.”</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div></div><div id="panel-reports-3" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="reports" tabindex="0" class="prose dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto [&amp;_[data-table-wrapper]]:![--page-padding:0px] [&amp;_[role=&quot;listitem&quot;]]:pl-4 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)_img]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child[data-table-wrapper]]:pt-0! hidden" data-component-part="tab-content"><span data-as="p">Tools that persist analysis back to W&amp;B.</span><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_2ibcljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Tool</th><th>Use when</th><th>Example prompt</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>create_wandb_report_tool</code></td><td>An explicit request to create a report or save findings. Accepts Markdown plus a <code>panels</code> array for line plots, bar plots, and run comparisons.</td><td>”Create a W&amp;B report comparing runs <code>abc123</code> and <code>def456</code>.”</td></tr><tr><td><code>log_analysis_to_wandb</code></td><td>Values computed in the MCP session (latency distributions, error breakdowns) need to be persisted as a run before being referenced in a report.</td><td>”Log these latency percentiles to W&amp;B as an analysis run.”</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div></div><div id="panel-artifacts-and-registry-4" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="artifacts-and-registry" tabindex="0" class="prose dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto [&amp;_[data-table-wrapper]]:![--page-padding:0px] [&amp;_[role=&quot;listitem&quot;]]:pl-4 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)_img]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child[data-table-wrapper]]:pt-0! hidden" data-component-part="tab-content"><span data-as="p">Tools that enable you to inspect and diff models, datasets, and other versioned artifacts.</span><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_2mbcljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Tool</th><th>Use when</th><th>Example prompt</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>list_registries_tool</code></td><td>The question is about model registries, registered models, or registered datasets in an organization.</td><td>”What registries exist in <code>your-org</code>?”</td></tr><tr><td><code>list_registry_collections_tool</code></td><td>To see what models or datasets live inside a specific registry.</td><td>”What collections are in the <code>model</code> registry of <code>your-org</code>?”</td></tr><tr><td><code>list_artifact_versions_tool</code></td><td>To list available versions of a model, dataset, or other artifact collection.</td><td>”List versions of <code>production-model</code> in <code>your-team/your-project</code>.”</td></tr><tr><td><code>get_artifact_details_tool</code></td><td>To inspect one specific artifact version, including lineage and files.</td><td>”What is in <code>production-model:v3</code>?”</td></tr><tr><td><code>compare_artifact_versions_tool</code></td><td>The question is what changed between two artifact versions.</td><td>”Diff <code>production-model:v2</code> and <code>production-model:v3</code>.”</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div></div><div id="panel-docs-5" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="docs" tabindex="0" class="prose dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto [&amp;_[data-table-wrapper]]:![--page-padding:0px] [&amp;_[role=&quot;listitem&quot;]]:pl-4 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)_img]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child[data-table-wrapper]]:pt-0! hidden" data-component-part="tab-content"><span data-as="p">Tools that answer product questions from the official W&amp;B documentation.</span><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_2qbcljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Tool</th><th>Use when</th><th>Example prompt</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>search_wandb_docs_tool</code></td><td>The question is how to use a W&amp;B or Weave feature or API. Proxies <a href="https://docs.wandb.ai" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">docs.wandb.ai</a>.</td><td>”How do I create a leaderboard in Weave?”</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div></div></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="schema-first-trace-queries"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#schema-first-trace-queries" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Schema-first trace queries</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">For Weave trace queries, call <code>infer_trace_schema_tool</code> first to discover available fields, then call <code>query_weave_traces_tool</code> with a precise column list and <code>detail_level</code>:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_c4ljddbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th><code>detail_level</code></th><th>Returns</th><th>Use when</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>schema</code></td><td>Structural fields only. Fastest.</td><td>Browsing or counting.</td></tr><tr><td><code>summary</code></td><td>Truncated inputs and outputs. The default.</td><td>Most analysis tasks.</td></tr><tr><td><code>full</code></td><td>Everything untruncated.</td><td>Drilling into a small number of specific traces.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">This pattern keeps token usage low for broad questions and lets the agent escalate to <code>full</code> only for the traces that matter.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="usage-tips"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#usage-tips" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Usage tips</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The following sections describe practices and workflows that help you get better results from the W&amp;B MCP Server. Start with the general practices, then read the section that matches your workload for more specific advice and multi-step tool chains.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="general-best-practices"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#general-best-practices" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">General best practices</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Follow these practices regardless of your use case:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Specify the entity and project.</strong> MCP tools need an explicit entity (your team or personal account) and project name. Include both in every question, for example “in <code>your-team/your-project</code>”.</li>
<li><strong>Ask focused questions.</strong> Prefer “Which eval had the highest F1 score?” to “What is my best evaluation?”. Specific metrics and time ranges produce better tool calls.</li>
<li><strong>Verify full retrieval.</strong> For broad questions such as “What are my best performing runs?”, ask the agent to confirm it retrieved all available runs rather than only the most recent ones.</li>
<li><strong>Combine with W&amp;B Skills.</strong> <a class="link" href="/platform/wb-skills">W&amp;B Skills</a> teach coding agents how to structure W&amp;B workflows. Skills provide patterns and MCP provides data access, and the two work well together.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="for-trace-heavy-workflows"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#for-trace-heavy-workflows" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">For trace-heavy workflows</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Follow these practices when working with Weave traces:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Start with the schema.</strong> Call <code>infer_trace_schema_tool</code> before <code>query_weave_traces_tool</code> to provide the agent with the valid fields and filter values.</li>
<li><strong>Pick the right <code>detail_level</code>.</strong> Use <code>schema</code> to browse, <code>summary</code> (the default) for analysis, and <code>full</code> only when drilling into a small number of specific traces.</li>
<li><strong>Chain <code>resolve_trace_roots_tool</code>.</strong> After a child-trace query, pass the resulting <code>trace_id</code> list to <code>resolve_trace_roots_tool</code> to map each trace to its root session in one batched call.</li>
<li><strong>Prefer <code>summarize_evaluation_tool</code> for evals.</strong> It aggregates the <code>Evaluation.evaluate</code> and <code>predict_and_score</code> hierarchy automatically. Only fall back to <code>query_weave_traces_tool</code> for raw trace data.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">For an end-to-end workflow, see <a href="#triage-failing-llm-calls" class="link">Triage failing LLM calls</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="for-run-heavy-workflows"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#for-run-heavy-workflows" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">For run-heavy workflows</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Follow these practices when working with W&amp;B Models runs:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Probe before you query.</strong> Call <code>probe_project_tool</code> on an unfamiliar run-based project to discover metric keys, config keys, and tags before constructing GraphQL.</li>
<li><strong>Use <code>get_run_history_tool</code> for time series.</strong> GraphQL doesn’t sample, so for loss curves and other time-series data <code>get_run_history_tool</code> is both faster and cheaper.</li>
<li><strong>Let <code>compare_runs_tool</code> do the diff.</strong> It returns config and metric deltas with aligned history in a single call, avoiding manual comparison.</li>
<li><strong>Run a health check first.</strong> When a training run looks wrong, call <code>diagnose_run_tool</code> before digging into history manually.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">For end-to-end workflows, see <a href="#diagnose-a-bad-training-run" class="link">Diagnose a bad training run</a> and <a href="#summarize-evals-and-compare-model-versions" class="link">Summarize evals and compare model versions</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="for-dedicated-cloud-and-self-managed"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#for-dedicated-cloud-and-self-managed" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">For Dedicated Cloud and Self-Managed</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Follow these practices for non-multi-tenant deployments:</span>
<ul>
<li>Prefer the hosted server on your instance at <code>https://[YOUR-INSTANCE]/mcp</code>. It exposes the same tools as the Multi-tenant server with no client-side <code>WANDB_BASE_URL</code> needed. Only fall back to a local install if the hosted server isn’t yet enabled.</li>
<li>When you do run locally against your instance, set <code>WANDB_BASE_URL</code> to your instance URL in the client’s <code>env</code> block. Without it, the server targets <code>api.wandb.ai</code> and the server returns no data.</li>
<li>Rate limits on Dedicated Cloud are separate from Multi-tenant. See <a class="link" href="/platform/hosting/hosting-options/dedicated-cloud/rate-limits">Dedicated Cloud rate limits</a> for defaults and how to request changes.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="for-local-installs"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#for-local-installs" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">For local installs</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Follow these practices when running the server on your own machine:</span>
<ul>
<li>Prefer STDIO transport for desktop clients (Cursor, VS Code, Claude Code, Claude Desktop). Only switch to HTTP transport when a client explicitly requires it (for example, the OpenAI Responses API).</li>
<li>When tool calls fail silently, set <code>MCP_SERVER_LOG_LEVEL=DEBUG</code> in the client’s <code>env</code> block and recheck the client’s MCP logs.</li>
<li>If you install from GitHub (<code>uvx --from git+https://github.com/wandb/wandb-mcp-server wandb_mcp_server</code>), <code>uvx</code> pins to the default branch. Pin an explicit tag by appending <code>@v0.3.2</code> to the Git URL when you need a stable version.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="recommended-workflows"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#recommended-workflows" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Recommended workflows</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Most real questions need more than one tool. The following workflows show common multi-step tool chains you can ask your agent to perform.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="explore-an-unfamiliar-project"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#explore-an-unfamiliar-project" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Explore an unfamiliar project</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To explore what has been logged to a project, chain these tools:</span>
<ol>
<li><code>list_entities_tool</code> to find an entity or team.</li>
<li><code>query_wandb_entity_projects</code> to find the project.</li>
<li><code>probe_project_tool</code> for run-based projects, or <code>infer_trace_schema_tool</code> for Weave trace projects.</li>
<li>A targeted <code>query_wandb_tool</code> or <code>query_weave_traces_tool</code> call using the discovered keys.</li>
</ol>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="triage-failing-llm-calls"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#triage-failing-llm-calls" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Triage failing LLM calls</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To find bad traces and the sessions that produced them, chain these tools:</span>
<ol>
<li><code>query_weave_traces_tool</code> with a filter on error or exception fields, and <code>detail_level=&quot;summary&quot;</code>.</li>
<li><code>resolve_trace_roots_tool</code> on the resulting <code>trace_id</code> list to map each failure to its root session.</li>
<li><code>query_weave_traces_tool</code> with <code>detail_level=&quot;full&quot;</code> on a small number of specific roots to drill in.</li>
<li><code>create_wandb_report_tool</code> to document the findings.</li>
</ol>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="diagnose-a-bad-training-run"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#diagnose-a-bad-training-run" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Diagnose a bad training run</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To run a health check on a suspicious training run, chain these tools:</span>
<ol>
<li><code>get_run_history_tool</code> to pull the loss and validation curves.</li>
<li><code>diagnose_run_tool</code> for automated convergence, overfitting, and NaN checks.</li>
<li><code>compare_runs_tool</code> against a known-good baseline run.</li>
<li><code>create_wandb_report_tool</code> with line-plot panels to share the diagnosis.</li>
</ol>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="summarize-evals-and-compare-model-versions"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#summarize-evals-and-compare-model-versions" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Summarize evals and compare model versions</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To find which model version performed best on an evaluation, chain these tools:</span>
<ol>
<li><code>summarize_evaluation_tool</code> for per-scorer pass rates and error counts.</li>
<li><code>list_artifact_versions_tool</code> on the relevant model collection.</li>
<li><code>compare_artifact_versions_tool</code> between the candidate and current production version.</li>
<li><code>log_analysis_to_wandb</code> and <code>create_wandb_report_tool</code> to publish the comparison.</li>
</ol>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="troubleshooting"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#troubleshooting" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Troubleshooting</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Use the following table to help you diagnose and resolve issues using the W&amp;B MCP Server:</span>

Regenerate a key at <a href="https://wandb.ai/authorize" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">wandb.ai/authorize</a> and confirm it is passed as a bearer token or set in <code>WANDB_API_KEY</code>.</td></tr><tr><td>Empty results for queries you expect to succeed</td><td>The team, entity, or project name is incorrect, or the API key doesn’t have access. <code>uvx</code> in Claude Desktop</td><td>Use the full path to <code>uvx</code> in the <code>command</code> field of external links may keep target=\"_blank\".\n   *\n   * @param {string | null | undefined} href - The href value from the anchor (e.g. getAttribute('href')).\n   * @returns {boolean} - True if the link is local and safe to open in the same tab.\n   */\n  function isLocalHref(href) {\n    // Missing or empty href: treat as local (e.g. placeholder or JS-handled link).\n    if (!href || typeof href !== 'string') return true;\n\n    var trimmed = href.trim();\n    // Fragment-only links (e.g. #section) are in-page and always local.\n    if (trimmed === '' || trimmed.startsWith('#')) return true;\n\n    // Compare case-insensitively so HTTP: and https: are both treated as external.\n    var lower = trimmed.toLowerCase();\n    // Local: relative paths (/foo), path-only, or protocol-relative that we treat as same-site.\n    // External: explicit http: or https: (and we do not change those).\n    return !lower.startsWith('http:') \u0026\u0026 !lower.startsWith('https:');\n  }\n\n  /** Chevron path matching Mintlify's sidebar group chevron (ChevronRightIcon, 18x18). */\n  var CHEVRON_PATH = 'M6.5 2.75L12.75 9L6.5 15.25';\n\n  /**\n   * Classes matching Mintlify's own sidebar group chevron (DropdownArrowIcon):\n   * its base ChevronRightIcon classes plus the sizing overrides the sidebar\n   * adds (w-2 wins over size-3 in the compiled CSS, giving the 8px width).\n   */\n  var CHEVRON_CLASS = 'size-3 transition-transform text-gray-400 group-hover:text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-600 dark:group-hover:text-gray-400 shrink-0 w-2 h-[1lh] -mr-0.5';\n\n  /**\n   * Replaces the arrow (external-link) icon with a chevron on a sidebar nav link.\n   * Matches the chevron Mintlify renders on expandable sidebar groups.\n   *\n   * @param {HTMLAnchorElement} a - The nav link element.\n   */\n  function swapArrowToChevron(a) {\n    // Only sidebar entries get the swap.
